# THERMIA EXPERIMENT LOG

## RC09F1 delayed TOP-UP feedback integration — PREPARED / NOT RUN (2026-11-04)

**Hypothesis:** same-epoch controller-origin 0864/4 TOP-UP active bit following one physically sent D25 pulse can provide delayed native activation feedback, independent of the transient 042E command-word comparator. **Baseline / prior evidence:** owner-LIVE RC09 W2R65 single TX 22:31:42.706, 042E returned zero, 0864 active rose 0→1 at 22:32:03.943; earlier live experiment COMPLETE / POSITIVE for native activation, additional heat not proven.

**ONE meaningful code change:** source-qualified 90s post-pulse observer added within existing single S92/RX engine, with 45s fresh inactive 0864 baseline, identical owner epoch, one D25 physical send, zero new UART writes/retries, existing ABORT. New read-only TOP-UP One-Shot Confirmation text sensor. Controller OEM summary now labels command word separately from runtime active bit. Integrates independent already documented RC08F1 D24 manual-only authorization root fix carried forward from later firmware branch.

**Expected:** new RC09F1 boots with NOT RUN; normal M6/CURRENT/CAL/HA unchanged. At a future independent deliberate user use (not a repeat test), after one D25 0→1 send and fresh same-epoch native 0864 OFF→ON within 90s, one native ACTIVE OBSERVED result; otherwise INCONCLUSIVE/INVALIDATED with NO RETRY. W2R66 command-word zero terminal remains INCONCLUSIVE as a persistent compare, unlocks calendar. No physical retry.

**Actual:** full private one-YAML ZIP assembled, strict YAML/AST/UI ID checks, one existing UART write, manifest and byte-exact W2R92 rollback PASS; original genuine XTR trace independently checked one ARM, one TX, 0864 0→1 after 21.237s. Target compile/flash NOT RUN, no new physical experiment. Status **PREPARED / NOT RUN**.

**ABORT/rollback:** existing Native ABORT or Disable Write User; original W2R92 flash. No new physical authorizations. **ONE NEXT ACTION:** owner flash RC09F1; normal RX/CURRENT/CAL checks, no repeat of verified TOP-UP.

## RC09 W2R65 TOP-UP one-shot native activation — COMPLETE / POSITIVE (2026-11-04)

Hypothesis: one bounded D25 0430/15 word2=1 pulse can activate controller TOP-UP runtime flag despite 0430 returning to zero. Baseline: user LIVE RC09, M6 stage40, native CURRENT4/4, single S92/UART/DE owner, 45s manual ARM. ONE change: manual ARM followed by one deliberate TOP-UP button activation; no firmware modifications, no repeat. Expected: one TX, live controller 0864/4 word1 bit 0x0004 rises, no retry. Actual: ARM 22:31:29.894; action 22:31:37.351; one physical TX 22:31:42.706; 0430 controller return zero and W2R66 inconclusive persistent-comparator terminal 22:31:43.305; native TOP-UP active 0 at 22:31:48.967 to 1 at 22:32:03.943. CRC_DIFF=0, RX drops0, FC16 receipts continue. COMPLETE / POSITIVE for native activation flag; real additional heat not isolated (ordinary DHW had already started at 22:29:32). Confidence: status edge PROVEN, semantic association STRONGLY SUPPORTED, added heating UNKNOWN. ABORT/rollback: existing Native ABORT and W2R92; no automatic retry occurred. ONE NEXT ACTION: source-qualified 0864 post-pulse completion indicator in existing S92 engine, no repeated physical experiment.

## RC08F1 D24 manual-only authorization FIX — PREPARED / NOT RUN (2026-11-04)

**Hypothesis:** existing RC08 W2R63 scoped trial permit is sufficient for exactly one novel D24 user edit; automatic W2R77 lease must never authorize D24 until controller semantic acceptance is locally demonstrated.
**Prior evidence:** owner-LIVE RC07F1 M6=5, CURRENT4/4, CAL12/12, V5 CRC/RX clean in excerpt; source RC08 contains old `routine_validated` D24 inclusion and `auto_d24` bypass. No live RC08 D24 test. Transport ACK is not semantic acceptance.

**Expected:** without fresh manual D24 ARM no D24 TX even with automatic lease. With fresh user-initiated ARM, eligible one-edit S92 transaction still requires controller-origin FC16 042E word0 and Thermia panel to claim POSITIVE.
**Actual OFFLINE:** YAML strict parsing and AST comparison PASS; D24 automated predicate false for 0→1 / 1→0, one UART writer, ZIP CRC/SHA and rollback PASS. No owner flash, no physical D24 TX or controller feedback. **PREPARED / NOT RUN**; earlier RC08 archive **SUPERSEDED / NOT RUN**.
**ABORT / rollback:** Configuration ABORT Native Session; restore old Hot Water Enabled on OEM display; firmware rollback exact W2R92. D24 permission not broadened to AWAY or TOP-UP. Keep firmware PRIVATE.

## RC08-D24 — bounded native Hot Water Enabled experiment — PREPARED / NOT RUN (2026-11-04)

**Hypothesis:** native OEMW/S92 descriptor24 can change controller-accepted Hot Water Enabled (0/1), via owned 042E/15 word0, with authoritative controller-origin FC16 readback, beyond the preexisting transport ACK evidence.

**Baseline / previous evidence:** last owner-LIVE RC07F1 retained M6=5, CURRENT4/4, Calendar12/12 and clean sampled CRC/RX; W2R92 semantic writes verified for existing parameter group. RC07F1 already contains D24 mapping, selector/ledger, one TX max and 042E source guard; the existing experimental W2R63 ARM was internal/hidden, therefore unavailable for ordinary explicit HA initiation. No claim that D24 was previously live validated.

**ONE change:** integrate RC07F2 UI cleanup; unhide only W2R63 as a disabled-by-default one-edit ARM; add a nonpersistent descriptor scope `w2r84_hw_trial_scope=24` on successful ARM (45s), checked before the existing preview/commit path and cleared after commit/Disable Write User/ABORT. AWAY/TOP-UP cannot borrow the D24 ARM. S92/UART/DE, FC03, FC16, cold-start and native read system unchanged.

**Expected:** ARM itself generates NO TX. On a user-initiated opposite 0/1 edit within 45s, after same-epoch/current-page and V5/no-collision checks, at most one existing OEMW 042E/15 TX attempt. Success requires fresh controller FC16 word0 and Thermia UI reflecting the desired value, not ACK alone. Restore previous setting on OEM display as needed.

**Actual:** no physical RC08 run or target compilation supplied yet. **Status: PREPARED / NOT RUN** (SOURCE / OFFLINE TESTED only). User compile/flash and a separate deliberate ARM+edit are needed for live result.

**ABORT / rollback:** `Configuration | ABORT Native Session` latches native TX lockout; `Configuration | Disable Write User` only at owner idle; restore original Hot Water Enabled using Thermia control panel, and flash exact known-good W2R92 firmware if needed. No room sensor removal, guessed reset, autonomous retransmit or new privilege escalation. Private full ZIP is not committed.

In those XTR profiles codes: **21=S 1N, 22=M 1N, 23=M 3N, 24=L 1N, 25=L 3N, 26=XL 1N, 27=XL 3N**; iTec XT codes 15–20 (10/14/16 kW 1N/3N). **CM930 3.0.0–3.1.1**: register **1340/0x053C**, ATEC 6/9/11/13/16/18 (codes 0–5). **Critical version-dependent reuse:** Connect **2.0.18** `info-2.4.1.json` uses codes **21–27 for iTec XTP 5/8/12/16 kW**, while Connect **2.1.105** `info-2.4.1.json` has no codes 21–27 and its **2.4.2/2.4.3** profiles use codes 21–27 for **iTec XTR**. ## Calendar UI manual evidence / physical-slot design (NOT RUN)

**2026-10-29 — Calendar planner UI constraints from user-supplied Thermia iTec XT / iTec XTR *Gebruikershandleiding*, §5.8 ‘Kalender’ (official manual evidence; DESIGN ONLY, PREPARED / NOT RUN; NO calendar writes).** OEM user manual describes **maximum eight calendar settings PER selected calendar function**, selecting one of four functions: temporarily block domestic hot water production; EVU/power-limit shutdown; reduce outdoor fan noise; reduce heating/distribution-circuit temperatures. Time function offers either **DATUM** (date period) or regularly recurring **DAGEN/WEEK**. Bold instruction: START **not before 00:01**, STOP **not later than 23:59**; an overnight schedule needs **TWO separate start/stop entries regardless of function**. HA planner should accept one logical cross-midnight period, present exact two physical settings (e.g. weekday 23:00–23:59 and next-day 00:01–07:00, with weekday mask shifted including Sunday→Monday), show occupancy per function and prohibit save if resulting physical count exceeds 8; support add/edit/clear with explicit individual controller-confirmed receipts and subsequent re-read; no automatic overwrite/compaction or second UART/DE writer. A two-slot logical group must be treated as a UI abstraction: controller has distinct physical settings, not atomic linked writes; if a second write fails, report PARTIAL and reread, preserve ABORT and rollback. Crucial **evidence tension**: prior local W2R29F1 live controller calendar READ displayed existing **Silent slot 2 00:00–08:00** even though manual says input earliest 00:01. Preserve the actual observed 00:00 in read-only display, never silently normalize/rewrite; implement 00:01 manual validation for newly requested entries pending exact OEM firmware/live interpretation. Do not infer raw empty-slot encoding or safe clearing protocol from manual alone. W2R29F1 stays latest locally verified user-write firmware; 51/51 earlier semantic writes closed. **CURRENT MILESTONE:** Calendar Write User UI/slot semantics design. **DELIVERABLE:** documented eight-slot-per-function and split-midnight UI requirements with discrepancy. **REAL BLOCKER:** genuine controller slot-write/clear mapping and receipt semantics not yet validated; no physical TX authorization. **ONE NEXT ACTION:** design exact calendar editor data model and per-function occupancy from proven 12/12 reads, then integrate source-supported bounded one-slot write/clear path in existing S92 engine; no repeat tests of proven groups.
